- **review-skills**: Reviews a skill or plugin that business users will use alongside {{PLUGIN}} (the organization's own, such as branding or document skills, or a third party's) for security risks and for conflicts with {{PLUGIN}}'s quality and security rules, and reports what to change. Use when IT asks to review, vet or approve a skill or plugin, or when the specialist reported a conflict with another skill in a pull request. ([grojof/eunomai-business](https://github.com/grojof/eunomai-business/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/review-skills))
- **review-skills**: Reviews existing Agent Skills against Anthropic's current skill-authoring best practices and Claude Code's skill rules, proposes concrete fixes, iterates on them with the user, and applies only the approved ones. Use when the user wants to audit, lint, clean up, or improve their project, personal, or plugin skills. ([jankorbay/skills](https://github.com/jankorbay/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/skills/skills/review-skills))
